<p dir="auto"><em>Official 5-Minute Technical Walkthrough recorded specifically for INSA Evaluators, Examination Committees &amp; CISO Reviewers.</em></p>
<hr>
</div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Project Mandate</strong>: An independent, multi-tenant, explainable, self-hostable cybersecurity detection and statutory incident-readiness platform engineered specifically for Ethiopian critical national infrastructure (CNI) and digital financial service operators.<br>
<strong>Institutional Submission</strong>: Official Project Submission to the <strong>Information Network Security Administration (INSA)</strong>, Federal Democratic Republic of Ethiopia.<br>
<strong>Regulatory Alignment</strong>:<br>
• <strong>FDRE Proclamation No. 1426/2026</strong> — <em>Critical Infrastructure Cybersecurity Proclamation</em> (Mandatory 48-Hour Incident Reporting to National CERT &amp; Forensic Chain-of-Custody).<br>
• <strong>National Bank of Ethiopia (NBE) Directives</strong> — <em>Financial Cyber Risk Framework &amp; Directive SBB/84/2022</em> (Real-Time Payment Rail Fraud, ATO, and Structuring Interception).<br>
• <strong>FDRE Proclamation No. 1321/2024</strong> — <em>Personal Data Protection Proclamation (PDPP)</em> (Zero-PII Storage, SHA-256 Masking &amp; Data Minimization).</p>
</blockquote>
<hr>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Evaluation Highlights demonstrated live in the video</strong>:</p>

<li><strong>Live Red Team Offensive Simulation</strong>: Account Takeover (ATO), credential stuffing, and velocity fraud executed on <a href="https://honorpay-banking.vercel.app" rel="nofollow">HonorPay Digital Banking</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Sub-Second SOC Detection &amp; Siren</strong>: Instant telemetry ingestion into <a href="https://ethio-cyber-shield.onrender.com" rel="nofollow">Ethio-Cyber-Shield SOC Console</a>, explainable risk scoring (0–100), and auditory situational siren dispatch.</li>
<li><strong>Autonomous Closed-Loop Mitigation</strong>: Zero-trust automated account freezing, IP blocklisting, and session revocation.</li>
<li><strong>INSA 48-Hour Regulatory Incident Dossier</strong>: Statutory compliance reports, Merkle hash-sealed audit logs, and chain-of-custody export.</li>
</ol>

<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-important" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-report mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Important</p><p dir="auto"><strong>Project Scope Notice — Minimum Viable Product (MVP)</strong>:<br>
This repository presents the functional <strong>Minimum Viable Product (MVP)</strong> of the Ethio-Cyber-Shield sovereign defense platform. It serves as a working technical demonstrator of the core closed-loop architecture: streaming telemetry ingestion, 4-tier declarative detection rules, explainable risk scoring (0–100), automated containment dispatch, and 48-hour statutory incident readiness.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Open-Source Distribution</strong>: The platform is licensed under <strong>Apache-2.0</strong> and is designed to be easily installable as standard packages across any Linux server, container cluster, or on-premise infrastructure.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Upcoming AI &amp; Machine Learning Expansion</strong>: While the current MVP enforces deterministic, 100% explainable rules for statutory audit compliance, upcoming releases integrate advanced AI/ML capabilities:
• <strong>Graph Neural Networks (GNNs)</strong> for detecting complex inter-bank mule transaction rings and smurfing across EthSwitch and telebirr.<br>
• <strong>Unsupervised Behavioral Anomaly Detection (UEBA)</strong> using Autoencoders and Isolation Forests for zero-day threat discovery.<br>
• <strong>Locally Hosted Sovereign LLM Copilot</strong> for conversational threat hunting and automated bilingual (Amharic &amp; English) INSA 48-hour dossier generation.</p>
<p dir="auto">📖 <strong>Read the Complete Strategy</strong>: <strong><a href="/Rammiso/ethio-cybershield/blob/main/FULL_PROJECT_VISION_AND_AI_ROADMAP.md"><code>FULL_PROJECT_VISION_AND_AI_ROADMAP.md</code></a></strong></p>
</div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Multi-Layer Detection Hierarchy</h2><a id="user-content-4-multi-layer-detection-hierarchy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Multi-Layer Detection Hierarchy" href="#4-multi-layer-detection-hierarchy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">ECS evaluates inbound security telemetry across four declarative detection tiers:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th align="center">Tier</th>
<th align="left">Designation</th>
<th align="center">Processing Latency</th>
<th align="left">Evaluation Scope</th>
<th align="left">Example Threat Scenarios</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td align="center"><strong>L1</strong></td>
<td align="left"><strong>Deterministic / Schema</strong></td>
<td align="center"><code>&lt; 5 ms</code></td>
<td align="left">Signature inspection, malicious payloads, header anomalies</td>
<td align="left">SQLi/XSS injection (<code>API-007</code>), path traversal, malformed JSON</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="center"><strong>L2</strong></td>
<td align="left"><strong>Statistical &amp; Velocity</strong></td>
<td align="center"><code>&lt; 25 ms</code></td>
<td align="left">Burst counts, temporal thresholds, deviation from baselines</td>
<td align="left">Credential brute-force (<code>AUTH-001</code>), high-velocity transfers (<code>TX-001</code>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="center"><strong>L3</strong></td>
<td align="left"><strong>Cross-Source Correlation</strong></td>
<td align="center"><code>&lt; 100 ms</code></td>
<td align="left">Multi-entity correlation, device-account divergence</td>
<td align="left">Single device accessing multiple accounts (<code>FRAUD-004</code>), IP watchlists</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="center"><strong>L4</strong></td>
<td align="left"><strong>Stateful Attack Chains</strong></td>
<td align="center"><code>&lt; 250 ms</code></td>
<td align="left">Complex multi-stage sequence correlation over time</td>
<td align="left">Account Takeover: Password reset + Device switch + Rapid fund drain (<code>ATO-001</code>)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5. National Context &amp; Statutory Compliance Features</h2><a id="user-content-5-national-context--statutory-compliance-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5. National Context &amp; Statutory Compliance Features" href="#5-national-context--statutory-compliance-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Designed specifically for the Ethiopian operating environment:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>48-Hour National CERT Regulatory Clock (INSA Proclamation No. 1426/2026, §18)</strong>:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Live countdown timer locked to the exact microsecond of detection (<code>Africa/Addis_Ababa</code>, UTC+3).</li>
<li>Milestone threshold warnings at 24h, 36h, and 44h.</li>
<li>One-click generation of the bilingual (Amharic &amp; English) <strong>EthioCERT Regulatory Dossier (<code>incident_report_v1.0.json</code>)</strong>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Critical Rail Safeguards (INSA Proclamation No. 1426/2026, §19)</strong>:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Mandatory allowlists preventing automated containment from blocking critical national settlement networks (<strong>EthSwitch</strong>, <strong>National RTGS</strong>, and core clearing lines).</li>
<li>Two-person dual authorization required for irreversible actions.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Forensic Evidence Vault (INSA Proclamation No. 1426/2026, §20)</strong>:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>SHA-256 Merkle hash chain across all telemetry, evaluations, and containment dispatches.</li>
<li>Any manual database tampering breaks the hash chain, triggering instant alert <code>ADMIN-005</code>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Local Financial &amp; Network Native Context</strong>:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Native Ethiopian Birr (<code>ETB</code>) formatting across all transaction velocity models.</li>
<li>Granular regional Ethiopian IP mapping covering <strong>Ethio Telecom</strong> and <strong>Safaricom Ethiopia</strong> subnets across Addis Ababa, Harar, Dire Dawa, Mekelle, Hawassa, and regional gateways.</li>
<li>Zero-PII data minimization complying with <strong>PDPP No. 1321/2024</strong> (automatic recursive masking of national IDs, phone numbers, and bank account numbers).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6. Verification &amp; Automated Testing</h2><a id="user-content-6-verification--automated-testing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6. Verification &amp; Automated Testing" href="#6-verification--automated-testing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

Universal System Integration</h2><a id="user-content-7-universal-system-integration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7. Universal System Integration" href="#7-universal-system-integration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To integrate any external banking system, core processor (e.g. Temenos T24), mobile wallet (telebirr, M-PESA), or payment gateway into ECS in under 15 minutes, refer to the complete <strong><a href="/Rammiso/ethio-cybershield/blob/main/INTEGRATION_GUIDE.md"><code>INTEGRATION_GUIDE.md</code></a></strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>⚡ <strong>10-Second Quickstart</strong>: cURL commands with automated HMAC-SHA256 signature generation.</li>
<li>📦 <strong>Supported Frameworks</strong>: Ready-to-use SDK examples for <strong>Node.js/TypeScript</strong>, <strong>Python</strong>, <strong>Java/Spring Boot</strong>, <strong>Go</strong>, and <strong>PHP</strong>.</li>
<li>🔌 <strong>Standard Webhook Mappings</strong>: Field adapters for EthSwitch, telebirr, and international payment rails.</li>
<li>🛡️ <strong>Closed-Loop Mitigation Receiver</strong>: Implementing the <code>POST /api/security/mitigate</code> webhook with idempotent rollback (<code>/revert</code>).</li>
</ul>
<hr>

Open-Source Package Ecosystem &amp; CLI Commands" href="#8-open-source-package-ecosystem--cli-commands"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Ethio-Cyber-Shield is built for direct installation across Linux servers, Docker environments, and microservice backends:</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installing via Package Managers</h3><a id="user-content-installing-via-package-managers" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installing via Package Managers" href="#installing-via-package-managers"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# 1. Install ECS Core Platform &amp; CLI via pip
pip install ethio-cyber-shield

# Or install locally in editable mode from repository:
cd Ethio-Cyber-Shield
pip install -e .

# 2. Install the Official Python Telemetry SDK (with Outbox):
pip install ethio-cyber-shield-sdk

# 3. Install the Official Node.js / TypeScript SDK:
npm install @ethio-cyber-shield/sdk"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 1. Install ECS Core Platform &amp; CLI via pip</span>
pip install ethio-cyber-shield

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or install locally in editable mode from repository:</span>
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> Ethio-Cyber-Shield
pip install -e <span class="pl-c1">.</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 2. Install the Official Python Telemetry SDK (with Outbox):</span>
pip install ethio-cyber-shield-sdk

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 3. Install the Official Node.js / TypeScript SDK:</span>
npm install @ethio-cyber-shield/sdk</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Running ECS via Unified CLI</h3><a id="user-content-running-ecs-via-unified-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Running ECS via Unified CLI" href="#running-ecs-via-unified-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Print platform version
ecs version

# Start the ECS Defense Gateway and SOC Web Server
ecs server --port 5000 --host 0.0.0.0

# Start the standalone Web Log Agent Collector daemon
ecs agent --ecs-url https://ethio-cyber-shield.onrender.com"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Print platform version</span>
ecs version

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Start the ECS Defense Gateway and SOC Web Server</span>
ecs server --port 5000 --host 0.0.0.0

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Start the standalone Web Log Agent Collector daemon</span>
ecs agent --ecs-url https://ethio-cyber-shield.onrender.com</pre></div>
<hr>

\x3Cstrong>Minimum Viable Product (MVP)\x3C/strong> of the Ethio-Cyber-Shield sovereign defense platform. It serves as a working technical demonstrator of the core closed-loop architecture: streaming telemetry ingestion, 4-tier declarative detection rules, explainable risk scoring (0–100), automated containment dispatch, and 48-hour statutory incident readiness.\x3C/p>\n\x3Cp dir=\"auto\">\x3Cstrong>Open-Source Distribution\x3C/strong>: The platform is licensed under \x3Cstrong>Apache-2.0\x3C/strong> and is designed to be easily installable as standard packages across any Linux server, container cluster, or on-premise infrastructure.\x3C/p>\n\x3Cp dir=\"auto\">\x3Cstrong>Upcoming AI &amp; Machine Learning Expansion\x3C/strong>: While the current MVP enforces deterministic, 100% explainable rules for statutory audit compliance, upcoming releases integrate advanced AI/ML capabilities:\n• \x3Cstrong>Graph Neural Networks (GNNs)\x3C/strong> for detecting complex inter-bank mule transaction rings and smurfing across EthSwitch and telebirr.\x3Cbr>\n• \x3Cstrong>Unsupervised Behavioral Anomaly Detection (UEBA)\x3C/strong> using Autoencoders and Isolation Forests for zero-day threat discovery.\x3Cbr>\n• \x3Cstrong>Locally Hosted Sovereign LLM Copilot\x3C/strong> for conversational threat hunting and automated bilingual (Amharic &amp; English) INSA 48-hour dossier generation.\x3C/p>\n\x3Cp dir=\"auto\">📖 \x3Cstrong>Read the Complete Strategy\x3C/strong>: \x3Cstrong>\x3Ca href=\"/Rammiso/ethio-cybershield/blob/main/FULL_PROJECT_VISION_AND_AI_ROADMAP.md\">\x3Ccode>FULL_PROJECT_VISION_AND_AI_ROADMAP.md\x3C/code>\x3C/a>\x3C/strong>\x3C/p>\n\x3C/div>\n\x3Chr>\n\x3Cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\">\x3Ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1. Signed Security Telemetry │ 3. Closed-Loop Containment Actions\n          (Logins, Tx, IDOR, ATO)   │    (Freeze Tx, Block IP, Kill Sess)\n                                    ▼\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                   ETHIO-CYBER-SHIELD DEFENSE PLATFORM                  │\n├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  • Bounded Stream Ingest (Redis Streams / Transactional Outbox)        │\n│  • Multi-Tier Declarative Detection Hierarchy (L1 → L2 → L3 → L4)      │\n│  • Real-Time Ethiopian Subnet Resolution (Ethio Telecom, Safaricom ET) │\n│  • Multi-Factor Explainable Risk Scoring Engine (0 to 100)             │\n│  • Reversible Automated Mitigation Dispatcher (Progressive Postures)   │\n│  • SHA-256 Hash-Sealed Cryptographic Audit Trail                       │\n│  • INSA / NBE 48-Hour Incident Regulatory Dossier Exporter             │\n│  • Multi-Tenant SaaS Architecture with Strict PostgreSQL RLS           │\n│  • High-Contrast Institutional SOC Web Console + Audio Sirens          │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\">\x3Cpre class=\"notranslate\">\x3Ccode>┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│             HONORPAY DIGITAL BANKING TARGET (targetsys)                │\n│  • Customer Banking Portal (Accounts, Transfers, Mobile Money)         │\n│  • Transactional Security Outbox (HMAC-SHA256 Signed Telemetry)        │\n│  • Edge Geolocation Forwarding (Vercel Edge / Cloudflare headers)      │\n│  • Closed-Loop Mitigation Webhooks (Reversible Account/Session Freeze) │\n│  • Built-in Red Team Attack Lab (/admin/lab)                           │\n└───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                    │\n       1. Multi-Layer Detection Hierarchy\x3C/h2>\x3Ca id=\"user-content-4-multi-layer-detection-hierarchy\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 4. Multi-Layer Detection Hierarchy\" href=\"#4-multi-layer-detection-hierarchy\">\x3Csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\x3Cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\">\x3C/path>\x3C/svg>\x3C/a>\x3C/div>\n\x3Cp dir=\"auto\">ECS evaluates inbound security telemetry across four declarative detection tiers:\x3C/p>\n\x3Cmarkdown-accessiblity-table>\x3Ctable>\n\x3Cthead>\n\x3Ctr>\n\x3Cth align=\"center\">Tier\x3C/th>\n\x3Cth align=\"left\">Designation\x3C/th>\n\x3Cth align=\"center\">Processing Latency\x3C/th>\n\x3Cth align=\"left\">Evaluation Scope\x3C/th>\n\x3Cth align=\"left\">Example Threat Scenarios\x3C/th>\n\x3C/tr>\n\x3C/thead>\n\x3Ctbody>\n\x3Ctr>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Cstrong>L1\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">\x3Cstrong>Deterministic / Schema\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Ccode>&lt; 5 ms\x3C/code>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">Signature inspection, malicious payloads, header anomalies\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">SQLi/XSS injection (\x3Ccode>API-007\x3C/code>), path traversal, malformed JSON\x3C/td>\n\x3C/tr>\n\x3Ctr>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Cstrong>L2\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">\x3Cstrong>Statistical &amp; Velocity\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Ccode>&lt; 25 ms\x3C/code>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">Burst counts, temporal thresholds, deviation from baselines\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">Credential brute-force (\x3Ccode>AUTH-001\x3C/code>), high-velocity transfers (\x3Ccode>TX-001\x3C/code>)\x3C/td>\n\x3C/tr>\n\x3Ctr>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Cstrong>L3\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">\x3Cstrong>Cross-Source Correlation\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Ccode>&lt; 100 ms\x3C/code>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">Multi-entity correlation, device-account divergence\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">Single device accessing multiple accounts (\x3Ccode>FRAUD-004\x3C/code>), IP watchlists\x3C/td>\n\x3C/tr>\n\x3Ctr>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Cstrong>L4\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">\x3Cstrong>Stateful Attack Chains\x3C/strong>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"center\">\x3Ccode>&lt; 250 ms\x3C/code>\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">Complex multi-stage sequence correlation over time\x3C/td>\n\x3Ctd align=\"left\">Account Takeover: Password reset + Device switch + Rapid fund drain (\x3Ccode>ATO-001\x3C/code>)\x3C/td>\n\x3C/tr>\n\x3C/tbody>\n\x3C/table>\x3C/markdown-accessiblity-table>\n\x3Chr>\n\x3Cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\">\x3Ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">5. National Context &amp; Statutory Compliance Features\x3C/h2>\x3Ca id=\"user-content-5-national-context--statutory-compliance-features\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 5. English) \x3Cstrong>EthioCERT Regulatory Dossier (\x3Ccode>incident_report_v1.0.json\x3C/code>)\x3C/strong>.\x3C/li>\n\x3C/ul>\n\x3C/li>\n\x3Cli>\x3Cstrong>Critical Rail Safeguards (INSA Proclamation No. 1426/2026, §19)\x3C/strong>:\n\x3Cul dir=\"auto\">\n\x3Cli>Mandatory allowlists preventing automated containment from blocking critical national settlement networks (\x3Cstrong>EthSwitch\x3C/strong>, \x3Cstrong>National RTGS\x3C/strong>, and core clearing lines).\x3C/li>\n\x3Cli>Two-person dual authorization required for irreversible actions.\x3C/li>\n\x3C/ul>\n\x3C/li>\n\x3Cli>\x3Cstrong>Forensic Evidence Vault (INSA Proclamation No. 1426/2026, §20)\x3C/strong>:\n\x3Cul dir=\"auto\">\n\x3Cli>SHA-256 Merkle hash chain across all telemetry, evaluations, and containment dispatches.\x3C/li>\n\x3Cli>Any manual database tampering breaks the hash chain, triggering instant alert \x3Ccode>ADMIN-005\x3C/code>.\x3C/li>\n\x3C/ul>\n\x3C/li>\n\x3Cli>\x3Cstrong>Local Financial &amp; Network Native Context\x3C/strong>:\n\x3Cul dir=\"auto\">\n\x3Cli>Native Ethiopian Birr (\x3Ccode>ETB\x3C/code>) formatting across all transaction velocity models.\x3C/li>\n\x3Cli>Granular regional Ethiopian IP mapping covering \x3Cstrong>Ethio Telecom\x3C/strong> and \x3Cstrong>Safaricom Ethiopia\x3C/strong> subnets across Addis Ababa, Harar, Dire Dawa, Mekelle, Hawassa, and regional gateways.\x3C/li>\n\x3Cli>Zero-PII data minimization complying with \x3Cstrong>PDPP No. 1321/2024\x3C/strong> (automatic recursive masking of national IDs, phone numbers, and bank account numbers).\x3C/li>\n\x3C/ul>\n\x3C/li>\n\x3C/ol>\n\x3Chr>\n\x3Cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\">\x3Ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">6. => new ReadableStream({ start(controller) {
	stream.on({
		next(value) {
			try {
				controller.enqueue(value);
			} catch (_error) {}
		},
		throw(value) {
			controller.error(value);
		},
		return() {
			try {
				controller.close();
